# 西游记（维基文库·百回） · 分镜（第 1–24 集）

## E01

> 18 段 56 切 · 共 201 秒 / 目标 180 秒

### E01-01 · 花果山水帘洞（正午透光） · 10 秒 · 3 个分镜

> 走位：水潭边群猴围成一圈，猴王在圈前最靠近飞瀑的位置，面朝水帘，群猴在他身后两步外驻足。

| 切 | 起点 | 秒 | 景别 | 运镜 | 配方 | 画面 | 人物 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| #1 | 0.00s | 3 | 全景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 花果山半山的水潭边，一群野猴围成一圈，你推我搡，谁也不敢往那道白练似的飞瀑跟前靠。 | 孙悟空 |
| #2 | 3.00s | 3 | 特写 | Push In（推） | — | 石猴从猴群后头挤到最前头，站在潭边叉腰，望着那道当空挂下的水帘。 | 孙悟空 |
| #3 | 6.00s | 4 | 全景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 他抄起一根树枝往地上一拄，深吸一口气，猛一蹬地，纵身朝那扇水帘跃了过去。 | 孙悟空 |

**H3 视频提示词**

```text
How the reference pictures align with the target video — Picture 1 (from Shot 1) aligns with the 0.00-second mark of the target video; Picture 2 (from Shot 2) aligns with the 3.00-second mark of the target video; Picture 3 (from Shot 3) aligns with the 6.00-second mark of the target video.

integrated_multimodal_description:
[Shot 1] Following <Picture 1>, a static shot frames a circle of jostling monkeys by a pool before a straight white waterfall in bright noon light, none daring to approach the curtain of water.
[Shot 2] At 00:03.000, the camera cuts to <Picture 2>: a slow push in toward the young monkey who elbows through the crowd to the poolside, plants his feet, squares up and stares up at the hanging waterfall with bright eyes.
[Shot 3] At 00:06.000, the camera cuts to <Picture 3>: static shot, a fixed shot holds the young monkey bracing a branch against the ground, sucking in a breath, then kicking off the ground and leaping straight into the falling water before the watching monkeys.

overall_soundscape: A roaring waterfall that swallows all other noise; the jittery chattering of monkeys and soft scraping of claws on wet stone.

non_diegetic_music: Lively bamboo-flute and plucked-string ditty, brisk tempo, a springing sense of eagerness.
```

### E01-02 · 花果山水帘洞（正午透光） · 9 秒 · 3 个分镜

> 走位：镜头从岸边群猴一侧推向水帘，孙悟空半身探在水帘外隔水望岸；群猴在岸上隔着水雾围看。

| 切 | 起点 | 秒 | 景别 | 运镜 | 配方 | 画面 | 人物 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| #1 | 0.00s | 3 | 特写 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 白水哗地把他整个吞了进去，水花溅起老高，岸上群猴齐刷刷吓退两步。 | 孙悟空 |
| #2 | 3.00s | 3 | 中景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 水帘里传出一声喊，石猴探出半张湿脸来，隔水朝岸上使劲招手。 | 孙悟空 |
| #3 | 6.00s | 3 | 特写 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 孙悟空：「里面别有洞天，都进得来！」 | 孙悟空 |

```text
integrated_multimodal_description:
[Shot 1] Following <Picture 1>, a static shot captures the white water swallowing the young monkey whole, spray bursting high as the monkeys on the bank all flinch back two steps.
[Shot 2] At 00:03.000, the camera cuts to <Picture 2>: static shot, a fixed close-to-medium look at the young monkey who pokes out half a wet face from behind the water curtain and waves hard toward the bank, water dripping from his fur.
[Shot 3] At 00:06.000, the camera cuts to <Picture 3>: static shot, a static close-up on the wet young monkey as he shouts across the water at the top of his lungs, `` <d>[Chinese] 里面别有洞天，都进得来！</d>

overall_soundscape: A loud splash as he plunges in and spray bursts; a muffled shout from inside the waterfall and the excited stir of the monkeys on the bank.

non_diegetic_music: The light bamboo flute continues, drums building toward the single plunge.
```

### E01-03 · 花果山水帘洞（正午透光） · 10 秒 · 3 个分镜

> 走位：水帘洞口为入口，孙悟空缩身钻回水帘；岸上群猴依次跳下，镜头随后跟到洞内石座。

| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| #1 | 0.00s | 3 | 中景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 喊完他一缩身，又钻回水帘后头，转瞬不见了人影。 | 孙悟空 |
| #2 | 3.00s | 4 | 全景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 岸上带头的野猴先壮着胆子扑通跳下去，其余一个跟一个，接二连三往里蹦。 | 孙悟空 |
| #3 | 7.00s | 3 | 全景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 他跨过铁板石桥，跳到洞中央一方天然大石座上，盘腿坐下，拄着膝四下打量。 | 孙悟空 |

```text
non_diegetic_music: Tentative plucked strings, pace slowing, a held breath as they enter the cave.
```

| #2 | 5.00s | 3 | 特写 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 孙悟空：「好，那我石猴就应下这个王。」 | 孙悟空 |
| #3 | 8.00s | 3 | 全景 | Static Shot（固定） | — | 群猴轰然欢呼，围着石座又叫又蹦，直蹦得水珠从头发上甩下来。 | 孙悟空 |

```text
integrated_multimodal_description:

overall_soundscape: The jeering of the monkeys rising in waves, then a noisy, ragged cheer with the patter of scrambling claws.
```

| E24-15#1 | 唐僧 | 善哉，一路的磨难，方是那八十一难的真正意义所在。 | 5.3 |
